# Roadmap

Updated: 2026-11-26

features:
- files: Open, save, convert and manage audio files (A)
- display: Waveform, spectral display, zoom and the Selection/View panel (B1-B3, B5)
- analysis: Level meters and loudness analysis (B4, G4)
- selection: Time, channel and spectral selection, and snapping (C1-C4)
- transport: Playback transport and scrub (C5, C6)
- editing: Cut, copy, paste, silence, crop, undo and crossfades (D)
- effects: One-shot effects, the Effects Rack and presets (E, F)
- repair: Noise print, Noise Reduction and Match Loudness (G1-G3)
- markers: Markers and the Markers panel (H)
- shell: Layout, panels, workspaces and shortcuts (I1-I3)
- platform: Autosave, the three operating systems, notices, memory, CI and logging (I4-I7)
- recording: Record a take (J)
- episode-check: Episode Check: findings, fixes and ready to publish (K)
- transcript: Local transcription and text-based editing (L)
- faces: Studio and Podcast faces, the Episode Map and the theme (M)
- automation: Automation core, command line, MCP server and scripts (N)
- multitrack: Multitrack sessions, mixer and CD layout (after 1.0)
- export-batch: Batch processing and extra export formats (after 1.0)
- plugins: Third-party plugin hosting (after 1.0)
- video: Video import and preview (after 1.0)

The plan from here to 1.0, rewritten 2026-11-11 from an audit of `main` at `11e49a5` and all 744 issues, brought up to date on 2026-11-16 from a second audit of `main` at `92d0f13`, and audited again on 2026-11-26 against `main` at `44bbfd5`, [docs/code-map.md](docs/code-map.md) and the pull requests and issues through #1632; each finding was verified against the codebase before it went in. The spec is [SPEC.md](SPEC.md) (the items of [docs/v1/v1.md](docs/v1/v1.md), sections A to N); the code map is [docs/code-map.md](docs/code-map.md); the stage is in [PIPELINE.md](PIPELINE.md). The old fleet tracker is kept as history in [docs/archive/roadmap-v1-fleet.md](docs/archive/roadmap-v1-fleet.md).

## How to read it

- **complete**: the code is on `main`, its tests pass, and the item's Done check is recorded where the row says.
- **built, check not recorded**: the code is on `main`, but part of the Done check (a measurement, a test or a by-hand check) is not written down yet. The steps say which part.
- **partial**: part of the item is on `main`; the steps build the rest.
- **not started**: nothing of the item is on `main`.
- A **step** is one change with one check. Its id is `<item>.<n>` (for example `C5.3`) and is the start of its issue's title. Stage 5b's and Stage 6's steps have no v1 item and are numbered `S5.<n>` and `S6.<n>` by stage (`D6` is the v1 item for zero-crossing adjust); the proposed Horizon items are numbered `P1` to `P6` (`P<m>.<n>`) and sit outside the 1.0 step totals until my ruling (Q13) so [SPEC.md](SPEC.md)'s approval holds. **Verify** names the test, measurement or by-hand check that proves it. A number dropped during the audit is not reused, so H1.2, J3.4 and F3.1 do not exist.
- **By**: `script` means an automated test or tool proves it; `hand` means someone runs the app; `hand, me` means only I can do it (by ear, or on a machine only I have). An operating system after it means the step needs that system.
- Sizes (`easy`, `medium`, `hard`) and kinds follow the pipeline's rubric. Each step gets one issue on the `1.0` milestone with one `feature:` label from the block above; the Issue column is filled when it is filed. A step's issue is filed before an agent takes it (AGENTS.md rule 2).
- **Start in** (under each item with steps left) names the files, functions and tests its steps touch, checked at `44bbfd5`. The code map's [Index by v1 item](docs/code-map.md#index-by-v1-item) lists every file per item.
- **Code merged (#PR). Left: ...** at the start of a step's What means the code is on `main` and only the part named is still owed. **Note:** at the end of a What means its Verify cannot pass as written without the change the note names.
- [Next up](#next-up) lists what an agent can take now, in stage order, and what waits on me.

### Critical-path risks & blind spots (found 2026-11-26)

1. **`L1.9` unfiled external dependency (longest pole to 1.0):** `L1.9` (Whisper base on `candle`) blocks `L1.10`, `L1.13`–`L1.17`, `L2.8`–`L2.9`, `L5.7` and `M1.12`, and waits on *"a shared Whisper crate split out of FilmCraft's speech crate (no issue yet)"*. In addition, `apps/wavcraft/Cargo.toml` does not yet forward the `whisper` feature (`L1.18`), hard 30 s window edges split words (`L1.20`), and editing a file mid-transcription currently discards the finished run (`L1.19`).
2. **8 GB Mac spectral refresh (`#1590`, `B2.9`):** running `tools/alpha/b2-refresh.py` (`B2.6`, `#1580`) on the 8 GB M1 Mac mini measured ~4,369 ms on a 1-minute delete inside a 3-hour WAV (limit: `<= 500 ms`) because `LengthFrom` rebuilds all trailing overview columns instead of shifting clean ones (`B2.9`).
3. **Preview & preset wiring gaps (`E6.8`, `F3.4`):** `WaveApp::toggle_effect_preview` calls `EffectPreview::start` instead of `start_at_rate(..., player.device_rate())`, so preview fails whenever the file rate (e.g. 44.1 kHz) differs from the output device rate (`E6.8`); and `PRESET save|list|delete` in `app.rs` is intercepted by conversion presets before reaching effect/rack presets (`F3.4`).
4. **Windows CLI subsystem & macOS TCC / Gatekeeper (`N2.5`, `S5.2`, `S5.5`):** `windows_subsystem = "windows"` silences `wavcraft convert|inspect|run` in PowerShell while naive `AttachConsole` breaks `wavcraft mcp` stdio pipes (`S5.2` solves both cleanly with a console `wavcraft.com` companion); unsigned ad-hoc macOS rebuilds lose TCC microphone permission or fail Gatekeeper on the second user's Mac (`S5.5`).
5. **Post-encode MP3 true-peak overshoot (`K5.6`) & 3-hour MP3 RAM (`A3.13`):** lossy MP3 quantization routinely pushes `-1.0 dBTP` PCM to `-0.5 dBTP` (`K5.6`), and `save_mp3` currently holds the entire encoded MP3 stream in RAM (~432 MB on a 3-hour 320 kbps export) and rejects 96 kHz documents (`A3.13`).

AGENTS.md rule 1 takes the lowest-numbered unblocked step of the earliest unfinished stage. **Ready** means every blocker is merged, nothing waits on my ruling, and a script proves it.

## Issues outside the step tables

These open issues have no v1 item step row of their own (except where S5.8 now adopts #60). They are tracked here so the weekly drift clear does not flag them.

- **#1291:** bump the soundcraft family past the `windows` 0.61/0.62 clash and drop the true-peak backport pin (#1284). After 1.0 by my ruling of 2026-11-12.
- **#60:** one-channel Paste near the end of a file (Audition 26.5 checked 2026-10-23: pasting past the end lengthens the file and pads the other channel with silence; pasting over a range keeps the whole clip). Now scheduled in `S5.8`.
- **#69:** hand-test of the viewer fixes and a real episode, listed on B1, C1 and C5. `docs/checklists/69-hand-test.md` has every box unticked, and its item 2 ("Space never toggles playback") is out of date since C5.5 and C5.6 (`S5.8` refreshes the stale wording).

## Stages

### Stage 0: the merge plan (done 2026-10-27)

The old branches went into `main` as 44 merge commits; the unmerged ones are kept as `archive/` tags. Its follow-ups are closed: `main` passes its own checks (#1012, #1010) and CI runs on every push and pull request (#1013). The rulings I made then still decide behaviour:

1. Save with metadata off drops markers and the INFO/BWF metadata, as Audition does.
2. Match Loudness (G3) has one implementation, `crates/core/src/match_loudness.rs`.
3. Dither (A4) is off by default, Triangular first, Noise Shaping None, remembered when on; an unedited 16-bit save is bit-identical.
4. Playback uses the read-ahead ring with the windowed-sinc resampler; the Dynamics effect already on `main` (`crates/core/src/effects/dynamics.rs`) stays.

### Stage 1: Editor

The store, the edit model, selection, transport and markers. I7's Mac measurement and its v1.md wording are recorded (I7.1, I7.2). D2 and D6 are not in v1.md's order of work; I place them here with the rest of section D.

**Done when** a full rough-cut edit of an episode is saved, reopened and identical.

**Complete:**

- **A1 Open WAV, AIFF, MP3, FLAC into the store.** Streamed open for WAV (all depths, BWF, RF64), AIFF, FLAC and MP3 is in crates/core/src/io.rs and store.rs; a file with more than 32 channels is refused before any buffer is reserved (check_channel_count in io.rs, MAX_CHANNELS in channels.rs). The window opens on a worker thread in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs. The status bar shows a spinner and a Cancel button while opening; Cancel and the control channel's OPEN_CANCEL line call WaveApp::cancel_opening. Tests: crates/testkit/tests/open.rs, crates/core/tests/store_and_doc.rs, multichannel.rs, apps/wavcraft/src/a1_open_cancel_tests.rs. ### Stage 1b: Automation

My ruling, 2026-11-03: "In v1, after Editor (Recommended)". Order: N1, then N2.1 to N2.3, then N3 and N4; N2.4 waits for N4.4. Once this stage is in, every later stage's checks can be run by script. Bounce from the command line stays after 1.0.

**Done when** an AI agent completes 10 real editing tasks through the MCP server with no window.

**Remains:**

#### N1: Automation core: one Backend, two ways (partial)

- **On main:** crates/automation is on main: lib.rs (Request parse and line for OPEN, SELECT, ACTION, EFFECT, SAVE, STATE; Backend trait), headless.rs (Headless over wavcraft-core), remote.rs (Remote over the control socket). Remote waits for a save and reports how it ended, Request::Save carries a path and options, Headless honours them and runs the one-shot effects by name, and a parity test runs the same requests through the window code (N1.1 to N1.5, N1.8, #1216). The window's control_request arms for OPEN, SELECT, ACTION, EFFECT <name> and SAVE call Request::parse, and app.rs's own parse_save_args, take_quality and take_meta are gone; the window keeps its own arms for a bare EFFECT (the list) and for ACTION window.* (N1.6). The window test of the SAVE options went with those functions; its cases are now crates/automation/tests/request.rs save_option_values_are_checked_as_the_window_checked_them. Tests: tests/request.rs, tests/headless.rs, tests/remote.rs, apps/wavcraft/src/n1_parity_tests.rs. Merged 2026-11-12: N1.1 (#1216), N1.2 (#1216), N1.3 (#1216), N1.4 (#1216), N1.5 (#1216), N1.8 (#1216), N1.6 (#1314). Merged 2026-11-16: N1.7 (#1373), the example crates/automation/examples/headless_window_parity.rs and the script tools/checks/1107-headless-window-parity.sh; its recorded run is still owed.
- **Done check:** The same steps give the same saved file, sample for sample, with and without the window.
- **Recorded so far:** In process only: apps/wavcraft/src/n1_parity_tests.rs the_window_and_headless_save_the_same_samples runs the same OPEN, four requests and SAVE through the window code and through Headless, and the saved samples are equal (N1.5, #1216). The run over the real socket is built (N1.7, #1373) and, under a virtual display for the PR, printed `same=1 frames=9000` and exited 0; that is not the recorded run. The run on a Linux handheld with a real display and its row in docs/checklists/N1.md are not recorded. N1.7 added no test name: it is an example program and a script.
- **Item issue:** #1107
- **Start in:** N1.7's code is merged (#1373): crates/automation/examples/headless_window_parity.rs runs the same steps through Remote::from_env (crates/automation/src/remote.rs; it reads WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT and WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_TOKEN_FILE) and through Headless (crates/automation/src/headless.rs) and compares the two saved files; tools/checks/1107-headless-window-parity.sh starts the app and runs it under timeout 180; docs/checklists/N1.md has the method and an empty table of recorded runs. Only the recorded run is left: on a Linux handheld with a real display, run `bash tools/checks/1107-headless-window-parity.sh`, expect `same=1 frames=9000` and exit 0, and add a row with the line, the date and the commit to docs/checklists/N1.md. The #1107 comment of 2026-11-12 names three traps: an open that fails while the open prompt is up can leave STATE at busy=0 before LASTERROR is set (WaveApp::pump_open_queue in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs), and an open the user cancels answers OK; the example checks the open with STATE for both. The third is that Remote sets no read timeout on its socket, so a stuck app hangs the script.

#### N2: Command line: run, convert, inspect (partial)

- **On main:** apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs parse gives Window, Run, Convert or Inspect. After a subcommand, an unknown flag or a missing operand is an error with the usage line; with no subcommand, an unknown flag is an error too (N2.1). wavcraft convert <in> <out> runs in apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs before the log and the window start, opens and saves through Headless, and exits 0, or 2 with the reason on standard error (N2.2). wavcraft inspect <file> runs in the same place: it opens the file through Document::open_in, prints each row of Document::properties as 'Key: value', and exits 0, or 2 with the file name and the reason on standard error; the document is dropped first, so no session folder is left (N2.3). wavcraft run <script> <file> [-o <out>] runs there too (run_script_file): it opens the file through Headless, runs the script text with Headless::run_script, and saves to <out> only when every line passed; with no -o it only checks the script and writes nothing; a bad line exits 2 with its line number on standard error, and no session folder is left (N2.4). convert, inspect and run all take --session-dir, before or after the subcommand, and a subcommand is found when it is the first word or comes right after --session-dir. So 'wavcraft --reset-preferences convert a.wav b.flac' opens the window, which tries to open a file named 'convert', with no message on the terminal, and also resets the preferences. crates/core/src/batch_cli.rs with crates/core/tests/batch_cli.rs is on main, but nothing calls it. Tests: the unit tests in apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs, and apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs, which runs the binary. Merged 2026-11-12: N2.1 (#1328). Merged 2026-11-13: N2.2 (#1333). Merged 2026-11-16: N2.3 (#1392), N2.4 (#1524). N2.4's macOS fmt job was cancelled twice by the runner backlog; I ruled that a cancelled job is infrastructure and approved the merge on the other 8 passing checks.
- **Done check:** Each command runs on a machine with no display; a WAV converted to FLAC and back is identical; a bad flag is an error with a message.
- **Recorded so far:** The convert half, with no display: apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs wav_to_flac_and_back_is_identical (a 16-bit WAV to FLAC and back has the same samples; the binary runs with DISPLAY and WAYLAND_DISPLAY removed) and a_bad_flag_exits_2_with_a_message (N2.2); apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs a_bad_flag_is_an_error_with_a_message and a_bad_flag_with_no_subcommand_is_an_error (N2.1). The inspect half, with no display: apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs inspect_prints_the_properties_rows (a 1 s, 48 kHz, stereo, 16-bit WAV prints 'Sample rate: 48000 Hz', 'Channels: 2' and 'Duration: 0:01.000 (48000 frames)', exit 0, no session folder left) and inspect_of_a_missing_file_exits_2_with_its_name; apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs inspect_takes_a_file, inspect_takes_session_dir_after_the_file, inspect_takes_session_dir_before_the_subcommand and inspect_with_no_file_is_an_error (N2.3, #1392). The run half, with no display: apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs run_applies_a_two_step_script (a 20000-frame stereo WAV, 'SELECT 1000 2000' and 'ACTION edit.delete' with -o: the output has 19000 frames, exit 0, no session folder left), run_matches_headless_run_script (the five-line script gives the same samples in both channels as Headless::run_script called in the test) and a_bad_line_writes_nothing (a bad line 2: exit 2, standard error contains 'line 2', no output file, no session folder) (N2.4, #1524). A Windows release build in a terminal is not recorded (N2.5).
- **Item issue:** #845
- **Start in:** apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs (parse, scan, Command::Inspect and Command::Run) and apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs (the dispatch in main, convert, run_script_file, exit_error). N2.3 (#1392) is merged: main.rs has inspect beside convert and prints the rows of Document::properties (crates/core/src/document.rs), and cli.rs gave Command::Run and Command::Inspect a session_dir field; run (N2.4, #1524) is merged: run_script_file in main.rs calls Headless::run_script (N4.4, #1484). Tests go in apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs. For N2.5: main.rs sets windows_subsystem = "windows" for release builds and nothing attaches a console, so a Windows release build's convert shows nothing in PowerShell today (S5.2 pairs a tiny console `wavcraft.com` launcher beside `wavcraft.exe` so subcommands inherit PowerShell's console cleanly without `unsafe` or corrupting `wavcraft mcp` stdio pipes). N2.6 closes the flag-order trap noted above (`wavcraft --reset-preferences convert ...`).

#### N3: MCP server (wavcraft mcp) (built, check not recorded)

- **On main:** The handler, core only: crates/automation/src/mcp.rs `handle(line, backend)` answers initialize, tools/list (N3.1) and tools/call (N3.2): each tool's arguments become a Request run on the Backend and the reply is the text, a Backend error is an isError result and an unknown tool is -32602. wavcraft mcp [--attach] is a subcommand (Command::Mcp in apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs; serve_mcp and serve in apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs, run before the log and the window): it reads standard input a line at a time, answers each through handle on Headless, or with --attach on Remote::from_env, and exits 0 at the end of input, or 2 naming WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT when --attach has no port (N3.5, #1407). The Backend it sits on is in crates/automation/src/lib.rs, and effects::all with effects::describe already give the effect list. Tests: crates/automation/tests/mcp.rs and mcp_tasks.rs, apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs. Merged 2026-11-12: N3.1 (#1248). Merged 2026-11-16: N3.2 (#1357), N3.3 (#1410), the ten editing tasks through the handler with no window, N3.5 (#1407), N3.4 (#1459), the same ten through the real window code (a WindowBackend over WaveApp::control_request that waits after OPEN and SAVE, with the edit crossfades set to none), and N3.7 (#1432), the checker: the example crates/automation/examples/ten_tasks.rs, whose `write <dir>` writes source.wav and the ten reference files 01-open.wav to 10-save.wav and whose `check <dir>` compares a folder of ten files with them sample for sample and prints same=10, or same=N differs=<names> and exits 1.
- **Done check:** An agent completes 10 real editing tasks through it, each checked, both ways (no window, and attached to a running one).
- **Recorded so far:** The Done check is not recorded. The handler's tools/call has tests in crates/automation/tests/mcp.rs: a_call_returns_the_backend_reply, a_backend_error_is_an_is_error_result, list_effects_names_gain and an_unknown_tool_is_error_minus_32602 (N3.2, #1357). The ten tasks with no window: crates/automation/tests/mcp_tasks.rs ten_tasks_headless (N3.3, #1410). The subcommand (N3.5, #1407): apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs mcp_answers_tools_list_on_stdio, mcp_runs_a_call_on_stdio, mcp_attach_without_a_port_exits_2 and mcp_refuses_a_bad_flag, and apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs mcp_takes_attach and mcp_refuses_a_bad_flag_and_an_operand. The same ten through the window: apps/wavcraft/src/n3_mcp_window_tests.rs ten_tasks_through_the_window (N3.4, #1459). The checker: crates/automation/tests/mcp_tasks.rs the_checker_accepts_its_own_files_and_refuses_a_changed_one (N3.7, #1432). The ten with a real agent, both ways, are not recorded (N3.6).

#### N4: Scripts: a text file of commands (partial)

- **On main:** crates/automation/src/script.rs has script::parse (N4.1, #1358): one Request per line with its 1-based line number, '#' comments and blank lines skipped, CRLF accepted, OPEN and SAVE refused, the first bad line reported as 'line N: reason'. script::run(backend, script) (N4.2, #1430) runs the parsed lines in order on any Backend, stops at the first error and returns 'line N: reason'; a script that does not parse runs nothing. History::squash_since(cursor, label) in crates/core/src/doc/history.rs (N4.3, #1356) replaces every step after a cursor with one step holding the current state and returns how many it replaced, or None when there is nothing to replace; Headless::run_script and WaveApp::run_script call it. Headless::run_script(text) in crates/automation/src/headless.rs (N4.4, #1484) runs a script with script::run; on success it squashes the steps the script made into one step labelled 'Run Script' and keeps the selection the script left; on a refused line it goes back to the starting history step, deletes the steps the script made, restores the starting selection and returns 'line N: reason'; a script that changes nothing adds no step. Headless::step_count counts the open document's history steps. The window has the control verb RUN_SCRIPT <path> (N4.5, #1486): WaveApp::run_script in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs refuses with no open file, while saving and while a background job runs, checks the file size (1 MiB cap), runs the lines through script::run on a private ScriptBackend, squashes to one 'Run Script' step with History::squash_since and answers 'OK replaced=<n>'; on a bad line it puts back only what the script changed (Redo steps from before the run stay), restores the selection and answers 'ERR line N: reason'. File > Run Script... in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs (`pick_run_script`, enabled when a file is open) opens a `.txt` picker and runs `run_script`, showing `line N: reason` in the status line on error (N4.7, #1563). Known limit (from #1484, tracked by N4.9): if the history disk cap drops its oldest steps while a script runs, the saved starting index shifts and `squash_since` also marks `Change::LengthFrom { frame: 0 }` instead of the union of replaced changes. Tests: crates/automation/tests/script.rs, crates/core/tests/history_squash.rs, apps/wavcraft/src/n4_run_script_tests.rs (7 tests). Merged 2026-11-16: N4.1 (#1358), N4.3 (#1356), N4.2 (#1430), N4.4 (#1484), N4.5 (#1486), N4.6 (#1530). Merged 2026-11-17: N4.7 (#1563).
- **Done check:** A 5-step script gives the same file both ways; a bad line stops the script, names the line and changes nothing; in the window one Undo reverts it.
- **Recorded so far:** Parts only. The reader: crates/automation/tests/script.rs comments_and_blank_lines_are_skipped, a_bad_line_is_named, windows_line_endings_parse, save_is_not_a_script_line, open_is_not_a_script_line and an_empty_script_has_no_requests (N4.1, #1358). The squash: crates/core/tests/history_squash.rs squash_makes_one_step, squash_keeps_the_steps_up_to_cursor and squash_with_nothing_after_cursor_is_none (N4.3, #1356). The runner: crates/automation/tests/script.rs five_steps_give_the_expected_samples (19000 frames; frames 0 to 499 are minus half the source within 1 LSB), a_failing_line_stops_the_run (the error is 'line 3: no action named "edit.nope"' and line 4's edit did not happen) and a_parse_error_runs_nothing (N4.2, #1430). The Headless step (N4.4, #1484): crates/automation/tests/script.rs a_failing_line_changes_nothing (bad line 3: samples, selection and step count equal those before the run), a_good_script_is_one_step (one edit.undo gives the source samples back), a_script_that_changes_nothing_adds_no_step and run_script_needs_an_open_file (13 tests in the file). The window (N4.5, #1486): apps/wavcraft/src/n4_run_script_tests.rs one_undo_reverts_the_script, a_bad_line_3_changes_nothing, a_missing_file_and_no_path_are_refused and a_failed_script_keeps_the_redo_steps. Both ways (N4.6, #1530): apps/wavcraft/src/n4_run_script_tests.rs five_steps_give_the_same_file_both_ways (the window with the crossfades set to 2 and 5 ms through PREF, and Headless with Crossfades::default(), each save a WAV of 2 channels and 19000 frames, and the samples are equal; the window answers 'OK replaced=3', because SELECT lines are not counted; the first four lines through a second Headless give a different file, so dropping a line on one side fails it; 5 tests in the file now). File > Run Script... in the window code (N4.7, #1563): `apps/wavcraft/src/n4_run_script_tests.rs` `the_menu_entry_runs_the_picked_script` and `the_menu_entry_needs_an_open_file` (7 tests in the file). The Done check as a whole is not recorded: no real window was run by hand (N4.8).

#### J2: No playback while recording (built, check not recorded)

The settings keep the dialog's choice: `dither = on` (Settings::dither, a bool) and the file-only keys dither_type (triangular by default, or rectangular) and dither_shaping (none by default, light or strong) in crates/core/src/settings.rs, written after `dither = on` only when not the default, so a default file is unchanged and an old file still loads (KEYS stayed at 46 with A4.3, and I2.1, #1521, made it 47; OWN_COMMENTS has 23 entries). ConvertDialog::restore in apps/wavcraft/src/convert_ui.rs fills the controls from the settings (a None type reads as Triangular) and ConvertDialog::remember writes them on OK, never on Cancel (A4.3, #1465). Save As… opens export_window in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs, whose Sample type row has the same three dither controls, a Dithering checkbox (off), a Dither Type combo with Triangular first and a Noise Shaping combo, enabled for the integer types and not remembered (WaveApp::export_dither, export_dithering and export_dither_type); the choice is used only when the saved depth is lower than the file's own depth: save_dither_for(doc, ty, choice) in crates/core/src/save.rs returns the choice then (depth_rank: 16, 24 or 32 bits, floats highest) and the document's own DitherSettings otherwise, so a save at the file's depth stays bit-identical, and save_dithered is save_aiff_kind with that choice (SaveOptions has a dither field) (A4.4, #1496). #### E6: Preview in every effect dialog (partial)

- **On main:** crates/core/src/effects/preview.rs renders an effect block by block with live settings (5 unit tests). apps/wavcraft/src/effect_preview.rs has the preview worker, EffectPreview (E6.1, #1393): a thread that owns a copy of the document, loops the selection through Preview in 1024-frame blocks into a MonitorRing and takes new settings over a channel (start, ring, set_params, error, is_running, stop); the control verb EFFECT_DIALOG PREVIEW starts and stops it (WaveApp::toggle_effect_preview and stop_effect_preview in app.rs, E6.2, #1446). The verb answers OK preview=1 or OK preview=0, refuses with no effect dialog open, no file open or a take running, and leaves the undo list alone; the preview stops when the dialog closes, when another dialog opens and when the document changes, and the player's monitor is cleared (a recording's input monitor is kept). Every effect dialog has a Preview button since E6.3 (#1501): EffectDialog::show in apps/wavcraft/src/effect_dialog.rs draws it in the button row, reading Stop Preview while a live preview runs, and a click returns Answer::TogglePreview, which effect_dialog_ui in app.rs passes to toggle_effect_preview (the E6.2 worker). effect_dialog_ui sets dialog.preview_on each frame from self.effect_preview.is_some() and takes the values every frame; EffectDialog::take_changed_values compares them with those last sent, so each frame in which the values changed sends one set_params message (a slider drag sends one for every frame it moves the value) and an unchanged frame none. A generator keeps its one-shot Preview of 2 s (start_preview in app.rs). The worker can resample to the output rate: EffectPreview::start_at_rate(..., out_rate) and out_rate() in apps/wavcraft/src/effect_preview.rs; a private Converter keeps the file-rate frames still needed and makes 1024-frame output blocks with Resampler at Quality::High, taking positions from next_out * ratio so there is no drift. An out_rate of 0 means the file rate, start keeps its signature and passes the file rate, and when the rates match there is no converter, so that path is as before (E6.4, #1467). toggle_effect_preview in app.rs still calls start, so the window does not pass an output rate yet. Merged 2026-11-16: E6.1 (#1393), E6.2 (#1446), E6.3 (#1501), E6.4 (#1467).
- **Done check:** Changing a setting while previewing is heard within 100 ms, by ear.
- **Recorded so far:** The Done check is not recorded. E6.1 (#1393) has four tests in apps/wavcraft/src/effect_preview.rs: e6_worker_loops_selection, e6_worker_applies_new_params_within_100_ms, e6_worker_reports_a_bad_selection and e6_worker_stops_on_drop. E6.2 (#1446) has six tests in apps/wavcraft/src/e6_effect_preview_tests.rs: e6_effect_preview_starts_and_stops, e6_effect_preview_needs_an_open_effect_dialog, e6_effect_preview_stops_when_the_dialog_closes, e6_effect_preview_stops_when_another_dialog_opens, e6_effect_preview_stops_on_a_document_switch and e6_closing_a_dialog_keeps_a_recordings_monitor. E6.3 (#1501) has two tests in apps/wavcraft/src/effect_dialog.rs: e6_every_effect_dialog_offers_preview (every effect of effects::all() offers Preview and a generator does not) and e6_a_changed_setting_sends_exactly_one_message. E6.4 (#1467) has four more in apps/wavcraft/src/effect_preview.rs: e6_preview_resamples_44100_to_48000 (a 1 kHz sine in a 44.1 kHz file read from a 48 kHz ring has 2000 zero crossings per 48000 frames, within 2, and out_rate() is 48000), e6_preview_resamples_48000_to_44100, e6_preview_resampled_level_is_kept (the peak stays within 0.98 to 1.02 and the ring has no overflow) and e6_preview_same_rate_is_not_resampled (the ring holds the ramp as it was). The by-ear half is E6.5 to E6.7.
- **Item issue:** #32
- **Start in:** crates/core/src/effects/preview.rs (Preview::new, render, set_params; Preview borrows the DocState, so the worker owns a copy), MonitorRing in crates/core/src/record.rs with Player::set_monitor in apps/wavcraft/src/player.rs, and Resampler in crates/core/src/resample.rs. Player::set_monitor refuses a ring whose rate differs from the output's, which is why E6.4 resamples first. E6.1 (#1393) added apps/wavcraft/src/effect_preview.rs and its four tests (main.rs declares the module); E6.4 (#1467) added its resampling and four tests there. E6.2 (#1446) added PREVIEW to the EFFECT_DIALOG verb in control_request (apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs; toggle_effect_preview starts the E6.1 worker and hands its ring to the player's monitor), where GENERATE_DIALOG PREVIEW and start_preview are the generator form; its tests are in apps/wavcraft/src/e6_effect_preview_tests.rs. E6.3 (#1501) changed the Preview button that EffectDialog::show in apps/wavcraft/src/effect_dialog.rs used to draw only for a generator. Next: E6.8 (script, unblocked), which wires `WaveApp::toggle_effect_preview` to `EffectPreview::start_at_rate(..., self.player.device_rate())` so preview works when a 44.1 or 96 kHz file is open on a 48 kHz device (`Player::set_monitor` refuses mismatched rates today); then E6.5 to E6.7, my by-ear checks.

#### G1: Capture Noise Print (built, check not recorded)

- **On main:** crates/core/src/noise.rs captures a print from a selection of at least 4096 frames and keeps it in memory; NoisePrint::save and NoisePrint::load write and read it as a file (magic WCNPRINT, version 1; G1.1, #1404). Effects > Save Noise Print... and Load Noise Print... (a .wcnp file dialog each; Save is enabled only when a print exists) and the control verbs NOISE_PRINT SAVE <path> and NOISE_PRINT LOAD <path> call them (save_noise_print and load_noise_print in app.rs; they answer OK frames=.. channels=.. rate=.. or ERR <message>), and NoisePrint::load refuses a file over noise::MAX_FILE_BYTES before reading it (G1.2, #1461). Shift+P is bound in crates/core/src/shortcuts.rs. Control verb NOISE_PRINT in app.rs. Tests in crates/core/tests/noise.rs and g1_noise_print_file.rs. Merged 2026-11-16: G1.1 (#1404), G1.2 (#1461).

#### G2: Noise Reduction (process) (partial)

- **On main:** crates/core/src/effects/noise_reduction.rs with Reduce By, Noise Reduction, two smoothing controls, decay, FFT size 512 to 16384 (4096 by default) and Output Noise Only, shown in the generic effect dialog with a section of its own under the controls (G2.6). effects::threshold_curve_db (re-exported in effects.rs) gives the per-bin threshold in dB that the processor compares each bin with, from a print, a channel and the settings (G2.5, #1406); the dialog's graph calls it. The section is apps/wavcraft/src/noise_reduction_ui.rs, which EffectDialog (apps/wavcraft/src/effect_dialog.rs) draws for the noise_reduction effect (G2.6, #1541): graph_data(print, params) gives the print line and the threshold line in dB, one point per FFT bin and of equal length (nothing for no print or an empty one), the section draws both lines and a Select Entire File button, and the button answers Answer::SelectEntireFile, on which WaveApp::select_entire_file (apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs) sets the selection to 0..total frames and the dialog stays open. Tests in crates/core/tests/noise.rs and g2_noise_reduction.rs. Merged 2026-11-16: G2.5 (#1406), G2.1 (#1403), G2.4 (#1426), G2.7 (#1468), G2.6 (#1541), G2.11 (#1531). Noise Reduction defaults to 50 %, as Audition does, with Reduce By 16 dB and decay 65 % (G2.1, #1403). Ctrl+Shift+P opens the Noise Reduction dialog without running the effect: Command::NoiseReductionDialog in crates/core/src/shortcuts.rs, handled in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs through WaveApp::open_effect_dialog, which the Effects menu uses too (G2.4, #1426). crates/testkit/src/lib.rs has nr_measure(clean, noisy, &NrSettings) -> NrMeasure (G2.7, #1468): it runs Noise Reduction on the noisy file, with the print captured from the longest gap, and reports floor_drop_db (the drop in the gaps), speech_change_db (the change in the speech windows, after against before) and print_frames; the clean file only marks which 100 ms windows are gaps and which are speech; NrSettings defaults to Reduce By 16 dB, Noise Reduction 50 % and FFT 4096. The binary wavcraft-nr-bench CLEAN NOISY [--reduce-db N] [--reduction N] [--fft N] [--sessions DIR] (crates/testkit/src/bin/wavcraft-nr-bench/main.rs, a [[bin]] in crates/testkit/Cargo.toml) prints floor_drop_db=, speech_change_db= and print_frames=, one per line; a wrong argument or a bad number prints 'wavcraft-nr-bench: <why>' to stderr and exits 1. wavcraft-nr-bench --time [--scale N] [--reduce-db N] [--reduction N] [--fft N] [--sessions DIR] (G2.11, #1531) needs no files: nr_time in crates/testkit/src/lib.rs builds a stereo 48 kHz file of 3600 s times N (default 1; N must be finite, at most 100 and give at least 4 s), written block by block, runs Noise Reduction on all of it at the settings (default Reduce By 16 dB, 50 %, FFT 4096), and the bench prints one line, nr_1h_stereo_seconds=<n>, and removes its working folder. Building the file is not timed.
- **Done check:** Measured on a test kit (clean speech plus recorded room noise, mixed at 10 dB and 20 dB SNR): the noise floor in gaps drops by at least the Reduce By amount within 2 dB, and speech changes by less than 1 dB. By ear: on a real episode a second listener and I prefer it to the untreated file and hear no underwater swirl at the suggested settings. A 1-hour stereo file finishes in under 60 s on the CPU of the Windows build machine (measured).
- **Recorded so far:** The Done check is not recorded. G2.5 (#1406) has two tests in crates/core/tests/g2_noise_reduction.rs: g2_threshold_curve_matches_processor and g2_threshold_curve_length_follows_fft_size. G2.1 (#1403) added two more there, g2_defaults_match_audition and g2_smallest_and_largest_fft_sizes_run, and made four tests in noise.rs pass reduction=100: noise_reduction_lowers_the_noise_floor_by_the_set_amount_and_keeps_the_tone, noise_reduction_does_not_depend_on_how_the_range_is_cut_into_chunks, output_noise_only_is_what_reduction_removed and stereo_gets_a_print_per_channel. G2.4 (#1426) added apps/wavcraft/src/g2_4_noise_dialog_tests.rs ctrl_shift_p_opens_the_noise_reduction_dialog and noise_reduction_dialog_command_with_no_file_open_is_an_error, and the row noise_reduction_dialog_ctrl_shift_p in the shortcuts! table of crates/core/tests/shortcuts.rs. G2.7 (#1468) added crates/testkit/src/lib.rs nr_tests::g2_measure_reports_floor_drop_and_speech_change: on a synthetic kit of tone bursts plus white noise at 20 dB SNR, with Reduce By 16 dB and Noise Reduction 100 %, the floor drop is 14 to 18 dB, the speech change is under 1 dB and the print is at least 96000 frames. In its pull request the test printed a floor drop of 14.10 dB, a speech change of -0.06 dB and 96000 print frames, so the floor drop sits just above the 14 dB lower limit. The bench has not been run on the real kit (G2.8, G2.9). G2.6 (#1541) has apps/wavcraft/src/noise_reduction_ui.rs g2_dialog_has_graph_and_select_entire_file (a print of 2049 bins at density 0.001 gives a print line and a threshold line of 2049 points each; the threshold equals threshold_curve_db and sits 20 log10 3.5 dB above the print; FFT size 512 gives 257 points and 16384 gives 8193; no print or an empty one gives no graph; select_entire_file(48000) is 0 to 48000 and select_entire_file(0) is empty); the dialog was not looked at in a running window. G2.11 (#1531) has crates/testkit/tests/nr_bench.rs g2_bench_prints_nr_1h_stereo_seconds (the bench with --time --scale 0.0027777778, a 10 s file, prints exactly one line, nr_1h_stereo_seconds=<number>) and crates/testkit/src/lib.rs nr_tests::g2_time_refuses_bad_scale (a scale of 0, NaN, 0.0001 or 101 is refused before any file is built). The real 1-hour timing is not recorded (G2.9).

G2.4 (#1426) added Command::NoiseReductionDialog to BINDINGS in crates/core/src/shortcuts.rs and its arm in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs; apps/wavcraft/src/g2_4_noise_dialog_tests.rs is declared from app.rs. #### F1: Effects Rack panel with Apply (built, check not recorded)

- **On main:** crates/core/src/rack.rs (slots with power, input and output gain, Mix, apply as one undo step) and apps/wavcraft/src/rack_panel.rs (a docked panel since F1.5, #1427: `rack` is in REGISTRY in crates/core/src/panels.rs, first in the right zone before History; RackPanel::show takes a Ui and panel_body draws it; Effects > Effects Rack… shows and fronts the panel; RackPanel::open is gone). Tests: crates/core/tests/rack.rs, rack_tail.rs, rack_reset.rs, using only invert, gain and de-esser; crates/core/tests/f1_rack_stream.rs has the four filters and Dynamics. Amplify, the de-esser, Dynamics (since F1.2, #1405: DynamicsRun in crates/core/src/effects/dynamics.rs is both the one-shot run and the stream, and keeps its envelope, look-ahead ring and gains across blocks) and, since F1.1 (#1408), Parametric EQ, High-Pass, Low-Pass and Notch (in crates/core/src/effects/eq.rs) have their own Effect::stream, which keeps its state across blocks. Invert, Gain and Channel Mixer work without one: effects::Stateless runs each block through Effect::process. effects::open_stream refuses the other 17 effects with an error ("<name> cannot run in the Effects Rack"; effects::NO_STREAM lists them and effects::can_stream tests for one; among them DC Offset, Normalize, Fade In, Fade Out, Noise Reduction and Graphic EQ), RackProcessor::new fails for a rack that holds one, and rack_panel::slot_effects (M5.1) lists only the 10 that can stream (F1.3, #1444). The gap F1.3 left, follow-up issue #1460, is fixed by #1466 (merged 2026-11-16, issue closed): Effect::stream returns Result<Option<Box<dyn EffectStream>>, String>, where Ok(None) keeps the Stateless path and Err makes the effect refuse, so DeEsser::stream_with returns 'De-esser (Multiband) cannot run in the Effects Rack' when its plugin is missing and the rack refuses it instead of copying the audio through; and the slot tests now catch a pass-through for every one of the ten slot effects. crates/testkit has the binary wavcraft-rack-bench (F1.6, #1497): it runs a rack of Parametric EQ, Dynamics and De-esser over generated stereo 48 kHz audio and prints one line, rack_3fx_1h_seconds=<n>, the time one hour of audio would take; --seconds N sets the length of audio it processes (default 3600), and a missing, non-numeric or zero value prints a message and exits 1. Merged 2026-11-16: F1.1 (#1408), F1.2 (#1405), F1.3 (#1444), F1.4 (#1443), F1.5 (#1427), F1.6 (#1497), and the follow-up #1460 (#1466).
- **Done check:** A chain of 3 effects applied to a 1-hour file in under 30 s on the Windows build machine (measured). Bypassed slots cost nothing.
- **Recorded so far:** Bypass: the slots_run_in_order_and_bypass_costs_nothing unit test in crates/core/src/rack.rs checks the output, and powered_off_slots_open_no_stream beside it checks how many streams run: 2 with two slots on, 1 with one slot off (the output is the other slot's, bit for bit), 0 with both slots off or the master power off (the output equals the input bit for bit) (F1.4, #1443). The refusal: crates/core/tests/f1_rack_stream.rs f1_open_stream_refuses_effects_that_cannot_stream (every id in NO_STREAM gives an error, and RackProcessor::new fails for a Normalize rack), f1_every_slot_menu_effect_changes_audio_like_offline (the 10 slot effects equal effects::apply within 1e-6 on 1 s of pink noise; since #1466 each is given a setting that changes the audio, where four of them left it unchanged at their defaults, #1460) and f1_rack_dynamics_does_not_allocate_on_wrong_channel_count, with can_stream_matches_the_list in crates/core/src/effects.rs. #1466 changed f1_every_slot_menu_effect_changes_audio_like_offline to set Gain gain_db=-6, Parametric EQ mid1_gain_db=6, Amplify left_db=3 and Channel Mixer l_from_r=50 (on a stereo input, since it passes mono through) and to require each slot effect to equal effects::apply within 1e-6 on every channel, to differ from its input by more than 1e-3 and to stay finite; it added a one-input, two-output assertion to f1_rack_dynamics_does_not_allocate_on_wrong_channel_count (it also passes on the code before F1.3, so it is a guard) and the unit test effects::deesser::tests::multiband_without_the_plugin_is_refused_in_the_rack; crates/core/tests/deesser.rs covers both stream paths. The bench (F1.6, #1497): crates/testkit/src/lib.rs f1_bench_rack_reports_seconds, f1_bench_rack_config_has_three_streaming_slots and f1_bench_rack_refuses_zero_seconds; on Linux in the cloud container `wavcraft-rack-bench --seconds 10` printed rack_3fx_1h_seconds=15.481 (not the Windows run, F1.7); a_release_rack_lists_no_dehummer_or_dereverb in apps/wavcraft/src/rack_panel.rs and the release test in apps/wavcraft/src/v1.rs now expect 10 slot effects (F1.3, #1444). The filters: crates/core/tests/f1_rack_stream.rs f1_rack_filters_match_offline_apply, f1_rack_filters_reset_matches_a_new_rack and f1_rack_filters_keep_channels_apart (F1.1, #1408). Dynamics: crates/core/tests/f1_rack_stream.rs f1_rack_dynamics_matches_offline_apply and f1_rack_dynamics_reset_matches_a_new_rack (F1.2, #1405). The panel: crates/core/src/panels.rs the_rack_is_a_registered_dockable_panel (F1.5, #1427), which checks the registry entry, that window.toggle.rack flips its open flag and that rack is first in the default right zone; F1.5 also moved the expected lists in the other layout tests of panels.rs and in crates/core/tests/settings.rs. The 1-hour timing is not recorded.

#### F3: Presets for effects and racks (partial)

- **On main:** crates/core/src/presets.rs saves, loads, lists and deletes effect and rack presets as files in the config folder, with unit tests in the same file. apps/wavcraft/src/rack_panel.rs has rack presets and a Presets menu per slot. The PRESET control verb reaches RackPanel::preset only for load: control_request in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs sends PRESET save, list and delete to the conversion presets first, so effect and rack presets cannot be saved, listed or deleted over the control channel. The effect dialog has a Presets menu (save as, load, delete) over the same files since F3.2 (#1425): EffectDialog::show takes an optional Presets handle, and RackPanel::preset_store gives the app the folder (set_preset_store, for tests only, replaces it). Merged 2026-11-16: F3.2 (#1425).
- **Done check:** Save, quit, reopen, load: same settings.
- **Recorded so far:** Save and load within one run: crates/core/src/presets.rs effect_presets_save_load_list_delete and rack_presets_round_trip. The effect dialog (F3.2, #1425): apps/wavcraft/src/f3_2_effect_preset_tests.rs a_saved_amplify_preset_loads_in_a_second_window, a_preset_for_the_wrong_settings_is_refused, delete_removes_the_preset and the_dialog_draws_a_presets_menu. Save, quit, reopen, load in the app by hand is not recorded (F3.3).

- **Start in:** crates/core/src/presets.rs (Presets::at, which takes a temp folder; save_effect, load_effect, list, delete, save_rack, load_rack). F3.2 (#1425) added the menu to EffectDialog::show in apps/wavcraft/src/effect_dialog.rs (with save_preset, load_preset and delete_preset), tested in apps/wavcraft/src/f3_2_effect_preset_tests.rs; the per-slot Presets menu in RackPanel::slot_row and RackPanel::preset in apps/wavcraft/src/rack_panel.rs does the same for the rack. Next: F3.4 (script, unblocked), which splits sample-type conversion presets onto `CONVPRESET` so `PRESET save|load|delete|list` reaches effect and rack presets over the control channel, and F3.3, the hand check on the Linux handhelds.

### Stage 4: Feel

B1 and B2 are not in v1.md's order of work; I place them here with the rest of section B.

**Done when** I work without hunting for things.

**Complete:**

- **B5 Selection/View panel and time formats.** selection_view_panel and time_box in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs, panel 'selection' in crates/core/src/panels.rs, TimeFormat (decimal, samples, timecode and more) in crates/core/src/view.rs. The control verb SELBOX <start|end|duration> <time> (header of apps/wavcraft/src/control.rs) types a time, written in the chosen time format, into a Selection box through WaveApp::apply_panel_box, the code the panel calls too after its no-document guard; no file open answers 'ERR no file is open', and an unknown box or a time that does not read answers 'ERR SELBOX <start|end|duration> <time>, written as <format>'. Tests in view.rs; scripts tools/alpha/f66.py and f83.py. Merged 2026-11-16: B5.1 (#1528). Done check: Typing a start and end selects exactly those samples. Recorded: crates/core/src/view.rs tests typed_selection_values_move_the_selection, typed_times_in_each_format, what_is_formatted_parses_back_to_the_same_text. B5.1 (#1528): tools/alpha/f66.py opens a 48 kHz file, switches to the samples format, sets the selection 100-1000 and checks that SELBOX start 150 gives selection=150-1000, SELBOX end 900 gives 150-900, SELBOX duration 500 gives 150-650, and, after SELECT 100 3000, SELBOX start 1,000 gives start 1000; a bad value and an unknown box are refused and leave the selection as it was. In the pull request it ended with RESULT pass on Linux under a virtual display. Item issue #19 is still open and can close.

#### B1: Waveform, amplitude ruler, vertical zoom (built, check not recorded)

- **On main:** Peak pyramid and waveform_columns in crates/core/src/document.rs, AmpScale and AmpZoom in crates/core/src/view.rs, ruler and zoom actions in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs. Tests live inside document.rs and view.rs; scripted check tools/alpha/f19.py. crates/core/tests/b1_click_visible.rs puts one full-scale sample into 4,800,000 silent frames and finds it at full zoom-out. Merged 2026-11-16: B1.1 (#1431), B1.2 (#1440), B1.3 (#1485), B1.4 (#1527): crates/core/src/frame_stats.rs summarize_frames takes frame times in milliseconds and gives a FrameSummary (count, median_ms, worst_ms and over_budget, with FRAME_BUDGET_MS at 16.7); B1.3 added p95_ms to FrameSummary (nearest rank, 0 for an empty list) and the app calls it from the FRAMETIMES control verb. The app times the work of each frame, from the start of logic to the end of ui (frame_timer_begin and frame_timer_end in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs), and keeps the newest 20000 values; a frame with the window hidden, where logic runs and ui does not, records nothing. FRAMETIMES answers 'OK n=<n> median_ms=<x> p95_ms=<x> worst_ms=<x> over=<n>', FRAMETIMES reset answers 'OK n=0' and any other argument 'ERR FRAMETIMES [reset]'; both forms and NAV are listed in the header of apps/wavcraft/src/control.rs. B1.4 (#1527) is tools/alpha/b1-frames.py (Python standard library only): it makes a 16-bit 48 kHz stereo WAV (3 hours by default, --seconds N for a shorter one), opens it with WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO=1, zooms to 2 seconds with NAV, sends FRAMETIMES reset (expects 'OK n=0'), scrolls with NAV for 10 seconds, reads FRAMETIMES, prints the raw answer and one PASS or FAIL line per check, and passes when n is at least 300 and p95_ms is at most 16.7; --selftest runs its parsing and judging on canned lines with no app.
- **Done check:** Lanes hold 60 fps while scrolling a 3-hour file zoomed in on the Windows build machine (frame times measured). A one-sample click shows at full zoom-out.
- **Recorded so far:** The Done check is not recorded. The click half: crates/core/tests/b1_click_visible.rs a_one_sample_click_shows_at_full_zoom_out (B1.1, #1431): at 1000 columns the click is column 488 and at 100 columns column 48 with a maximum of 1.0, and every other column is (0.0, 0.0). B1.2 (#1440) has crates/core/tests/b1_frame_stats.rs summary_of_five_frames ([10, 12, 14, 16, 100] gives count 5, median 14.0, worst 100.0 and 1 over), an_empty_list_is_all_zero, an_even_count_takes_the_mean_of_the_middle_two, a_frame_of_exactly_16_7_is_not_over and values_that_are_not_finite_are_skipped. B1.3 (#1485) adds p95_is_the_nearest_rank and p95_of_one_frame_is_that_frame there, and apps/wavcraft/src/b1_frametimes_tests.rs has reset_answers_n_zero, a_timed_frame_is_counted, ending_without_a_start_records_nothing, the_list_keeps_only_the_newest_frames and an_unknown_argument_is_refused. B1.4 (#1527): tools/alpha/b1-frames.py --selftest passes 8 of 8, and a run in the pull request on Linux under a virtual display with --seconds 120 printed PASS on all 7 checks and the line 'OK n=426 median_ms=1.84 p95_ms=2.74 worst_ms=7.00 over=0'. That is a 2-minute file in the cloud container, not the Done check. The 60 fps half, on the 3-hour file on the Windows build machine, is not recorded (B1.5).

#### B2: Spectral Frequency Display toggle, keys, refresh (partial)

- **On main:** Drawn display, divider drag and double-click toggle (apps/wavcraft/src/app/spectral.rs), resolution keys Ctrl+Shift+Up/Down, three scales through the View menu and the SPECTRAL verb, incremental refresh (crates/core/src/spectrogram.rs). Tests: crates/core/tests/incremental_overviews.rs, crates/core/tests/shortcuts.rs. Shift+D and F9 run ActionId::ViewSpectralToggle (view.spectral.toggle, "Spectral Frequency Display"), which run_window_action answers by flipping the spectral panel (B2.1, #1456); its two rows are in the locked Help-list test. Scale::more_log and Scale::more_linear in crates/core/src/freq_axis.rs take one step along Linear, LogPartial and LogFull and stop at each end (B2.2, #1429). Four actions call them (B2.3, #1519): view.spectral.scaleMoreLog, scaleMoreLinear, scaleFullLog and scaleFullLinear (ActionId::SpectralScaleMoreLog and the three like it in crates/core/src/actions.rs), bound to Ctrl+Alt+Up, Ctrl+Alt+Down, Ctrl+Alt+PageUp and Ctrl+Alt+PageDown in BINDINGS; WaveApp::step_freq_scale in apps/wavcraft/src/app/spectral.rs moves the scale one step (more log, more linear) or sets it fully log or fully linear, and calls set_freq_axis. Their four rows are in the locked Help-list test, as my leave of Q11 allows. The toolbar has the Spectral toggle (view.spectral.toggle, glyph 'Spectral') straight after Snap (B2.4, #1480); it has no pressed state yet. ACTION view.spectral.resolutionUp and resolutionDown now answer OK after they change the resolution (they answered ERR after working; fixed by the tidy-up #1548, issue #1526). SPECTRAL ends its OK answer with edit_to_picture_ms=none, pending or a whole number (its view, scale, scroll and reset forms answer with the band alone): EditToPicture in apps/wavcraft/src/app/spectral.rs starts its clock at a document change, learns the generation of the changed document when it is ready, and stops at the first spectral picture of that generation; opening or closing a file resets it to none (B2.5, #1543). tools/alpha/b2-refresh.py (B2.6, #1580) opens a 3-hour 48 kHz stereo WAV, deletes one minute from the middle, undoes and redoes, and polls SPECTRAL until `edit_to_picture_ms` is a number; on the 8 GB M1 Mac mini it measured ~4,369 ms on the delete (over the 500 ms limit) because `LengthFrom` recomputes every trailing spectrogram overview column and peak block (#1581, #1590). Merged 2026-11-16: B2.1 (#1456), B2.2 (#1429), B2.4 (#1480), B2.3 (#1519), B2.5 (#1543). Merged 2026-11-18: B2.6 (#1580).

B2.2 (#1429) has crates/core/tests/b2_scale_steps.rs the_scale_steps_and_stops. B2.3 (#1519) has apps/wavcraft/src/b2_3_freq_scale_tests.rs the_four_keys_move_the_frequency_scale (in a window with a 3 s file, started at Linear: ACTION view.spectral.scaleMoreLog gives LogPartial, then LogFull, then stays LogFull; scaleMoreLinear steps back to LogPartial, Linear and stays Linear; scaleFullLog gives LogFull; scaleFullLinear gives Linear; and KEY Ctrl+Alt+PageUp gives LogFull; each answer is OK and the scale word in STATE follows), four rows in crates/core/tests/shortcuts.rs (scale_more_log_ctrl_alt_up, scale_more_linear_ctrl_alt_down, scale_full_log_ctrl_alt_page_up and scale_full_linear_ctrl_alt_page_down) and four rows in the fixed list of apps/wavcraft/src/i3_shortcut_help_tests.rs. The bars, the hold line and the scale ticks take their x positions and colour crossovers from wavcraft_core::meters::levels_scale (bar_fraction, YELLOW_FROM_DB, RED_FROM_DB) through one bar_x(lane, db) helper, and the AMBER_FROM and RED_FROM constants are gone (B4.2, #1487). LevelTap carries lock-free per-channel energy (`record_energy`, `take_energy`, B4.3 #1500, overflow-guarded in #1548), `player.rs` feeds it at both playback meter points, and `MetersUi::update` feeds `RmsLevels::feed_energy` each frame and reports `l_rms` and `r_rms` in `METERS` (B4.4 #1529, B4.5 #1575; script `tools/alpha/b4-rms.py`). Tests: `peak_modes.rs`, `rms_reading.rs`, `b4_levels_scale.rs`, `b4_levels_tone.rs`, `b4_meter_matches_dsp.rs`, `b4_rms_tap.rs`. Merged 2026-11-16: B4.1 (#1481), B4.2 (#1487), B4.3 (#1500), B4.8 (#1499), B4.4 (#1529). Merged 2026-11-17: B4.5 (#1575).
- **Done check:** Matches the dsp crate's meter on a test tone to 0.1 dB. Shows while playing.
- **Recorded so far:** The Done check is not recorded as a whole. Peak, to 0.1 dB: crates/core/tests/b4_levels_tone.rs a_minus_20_dbfs_tone_reads_minus_20_db (B4.1, #1481) calls Levels::update([p, p], 0.01) once per 480-frame block of a 1 kHz sine of amplitude 0.1 at 48 kHz, p being the block's peak, for 100 blocks, and both channels read -20.0 dB within 0.1. RMS against the dsp crate: crates/core/tests/b4_meter_matches_dsp.rs rms_matches_the_dsp_meter_on_a_tone (B4.8, #1499) feeds 3 s of the same tone to RmsLevels::new(48000.0, 300.0) and to soundcraft_dsp::meter::RmsMeter::new(48000.0); both read -23.01 dB within 0.1 and differ by at most 0.1. The panel: apps/wavcraft/src/meters_ui.rs the_bar_uses_the_core_scale (B4.2, #1487) puts -60, -30, -18, -6 and 0 dB at x 0, 300, 420, 540 and 600 in a 600-point lane. The tap: crates/core/tests/b4_rms_tap.rs energy_adds_up_and_take_starts_over and bad_energy_is_ignored_and_peaks_are_separate (B4.3, #1500). 'Shows while playing' is not recorded; B4.7 does it. The f26.py run in B4.2's pull request (#1487) failed with 'open timed out' in the cloud container (no sound card), and it fails the same way on the code before the change. That run is not part of B4.2's Verify (a grep and the unit test, both met), so nothing is owed for it. B4.4 (#1529) added crates/core/tests/b4_rms_tap.rs a_block_of_half_power_reads_minus_three_db (RmsLevels::new(48000.0, 300.0); feed_energy(0, 7200.0, 14400) reads -3.01 dB within 0.01, then feed_energy(0, 0.0, 14400) reads the floor) and a_block_longer_than_the_window_and_bad_numbers_are_safe (NaN, a negative sum and a zero frame count leave the floor; a sum of 1e300 over one frame reads a finite value; 14,400 of energy over a million frames reads -18.42 dB within 0.01). The tidy-up #1548 added `crates/core/tests/b4_rms_tap.rs` `an_overflowing_sum_counts_no_frames`. B4.5 (#1575) wired `record_energy` in `player.rs`, `RmsLevels` in `meters_ui.rs` and `tools/alpha/b4-rms.py` (which passed under a virtual display with `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO=1` in #1575).

#### C4: Spectral selection: Marquee and Lasso (partial)

- **On main:** Marquee tool on key E, drag and move, Band and SpectralSelection with a raised-cosine mask (crates/core/src/view.rs), drawn band and GESTURE marquee verb in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs. The Lasso tool is live (C4.6, #1547): ActionId::ToolLasso ('tool.lasso', 'Lasso Selection Tool', a window action) in crates/core/src/actions.rs is bound to plain D in crates/core/src/shortcuts.rs (Ctrl+D is still Deselect All and Shift+D the spectral toggle), Tool::Lasso (name lasso) is in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs, and the tool.lasso entry of TOOLS in apps/wavcraft/src/menus.rs is on the toolbar and in the Tools panel (Paintbrush and Spot Healing stay planned). Until C4.7 builds the drag, a Lasso click or drag acts like the Time Selection tool (Tool::selects_time). LassoSelection in crates/core/src/view.rs (C4.5, #1498) is a closed outline of (frame, Hz) points with the Band feather fields (feather_hz, feather_ms) and rate: LassoSelection::new cleans its values as Band::new does, and mask(frame, hz) is 1 inside or on the outline and a raised-cosine fade outside it, measured in feather widths; fewer than 3 points select nothing. It is not re-exported from lib.rs and nothing in the app uses it (C4.7 does). blend_in_band(&Stft, untouched, processed, first_frame, rate, mask) in crates/core/src/stft.rs returns the untouched audio plus the short-time spectrum of processed minus untouched, scaled bin by bin by mask(frame, hz) (0 to 1, read at the middle of each STFT frame; a value that is not a number counts as 0); where the mask is 0 the output is the untouched input (C4.1, #1525). New marquees carry a default feather (`DEFAULT_FEATHER_HZ` 50.0, `DEFAULT_FEATHER_MS` 20.0 in `view.rs`, C4.2 #1595), and `Editor::run_effect_in_band` in `crates/core/src/actions.rs` runs any one-shot effect on the feather-widened time range, blends through `stft::blend_in_band` and pushes one History step named after the effect (C4.3, #1571). Tests: `spectral_selection.rs`, `c4_lasso_mask.rs`, `c4_spectral_blend.rs` (5 tests), `c4_effect_in_band.rs`. Merged 2026-11-16: C4.5 (#1498), C4.1 (#1525), C4.6 (#1547). Merged 2026-11-17/19: C4.3 (#1571), C4.2 (#1595).
- **Done check:** Amplify -20 dB on a marquee round a 1 kHz tone in pink noise lowers the tone 20 +/- 1 dB, noise outside changes under 0.5 dB; feathered edges, no click.
- **Recorded so far:** The Done check is not recorded. C4.5 (#1498) has crates/core/tests/c4_lasso_mask.rs a_rectangle_lasso_equals_the_marquee, a_triangle_knows_inside_from_outside, a_feather_fades_outside_the_outline and too_few_points_select_nothing. C4.1 (#1525) has crates/core/tests/c4_spectral_blend.rs the_tone_drops_and_the_noise_stays (5 s at 48 kHz, seeded pink noise at -30 dBFS plus 1 kHz at -12 dBFS, processed is input times 0.1, box 2.0-3.0 s and 900-1100 Hz: the 1 kHz level drops 20 dB within 1, the 2-8 kHz power changes under 0.5 dB and samples before 1.9 s are bit-identical), unmasked_blend_returns_the_input, a_full_mask_returns_the_processed_audio and a_length_mismatch_is_an_error. C4.6 (#1547) has crates/core/tests/shortcuts.rs tool_lasso_d ('D' resolves to ActionId::ToolLasso) and tool_lasso_d_leaves_ctrl_d_alone (Ctrl+D is DeselectAll, D is ToolLasso), apps/wavcraft/src/menus.rs the_lasso_is_on_the_toolbar (toolbar_item("tool.lasso") is Cmd::Tool(Tool::Lasso)) and apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs the_lasso_selects_time_like_the_select_tool; in the running app (Linux, a release build under a virtual display) 'ACTION tool.lasso' answered OK and STATE then showed tool=lasso, and tools/alpha/f82.py passed 18 of 18 (its planned-tools check now looks for tool.paintbrush:0). C4.2 (#1595) added `crates/core/tests/c4_spectral_blend.rs` `the_default_feather_leaves_no_click`. C4.3 (#1571) added `crates/core/tests/c4_effect_in_band.rs` `amplify_in_a_band_is_one_undo_step` (Amplify -20 dB in box 2.0-3.0 s, 900-1100 Hz lowers 1 kHz by 20 +/- 1 dB as one `Amplify` History step, and Undo restores bit-identical samples). App wiring (C4.4) and Lasso drag (C4.7) are not recorded yet.
- **Item issue:** #23
- **Start in:** C4.1 (#1525) and C4.5 (#1498) are merged; C4.2 (#1595) and C4.3 (#1571) are merged, so C4.4 is unblocked now: it sends `WaveApp::run_effect`, `finish_effect_dialog` and the `EFFECT` verb in `apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs` through `Editor::run_effect_in_band` when a spectral selection is live, and writes `tools/alpha/c4-band.sh`. C4.7 adds `GESTURE lasso` and the outline drag; C4.8 records the release-build run in `docs/checklists/C4.md`.

### Stage 4b: Episode Check, transcript and faces

My ruling, 2026-11-01: "Into v1". Order: M3 and M5 first; K1, K2 and L1 can start alongside; K3 to K5 after stage 3's tools exist; then M2, M1 and M4. M4's last steps and M2.8 follow M1, since M1 needs the map, the findings panel and the transcript panel. M1.12 (on a Mac) is taken in stage 5.

**Done when** the Podcast face's user cleans a real episode in it without help.

**Complete:**

- **K1 Run Episode Check with progress and Cancel.** `check::scan_with` and `check::scan_doc` in `crates/core/src/check/mod.rs` stream from the store with progress and cancellation; `CheckRun` in `apps/wavcraft/src/check_panel.rs` runs on a worker thread, started by `ActionId::EpisodeCheck` (`check.run`) from Effects > Run Episode Check and the `Check` toolbar button (`menus::effects_extra` and `toolbar`, K1.7 #1569), with progress, Cancel and stale-report status in the status bar and `CHECK run|cancel|state` on the control channel (`tools/alpha/k1.py`, K1.6 #1319). Merged 2026-11-12: K1.1–K1.5, K1.8 (#1208); K1.6 (#1319). Merged 2026-11-17: K1.7 (#1569). Merged 2026-11-19: K1.9 (#1601). Done check: 1-hour 48 kHz stereo file scans in under 60 s on the 64 GB Linux tablet, adds at most 200 MB of memory (both measured); Cancel stops within 1 s. Recorded: `docs/checklists/K1.md` (64 GB Linux tablet, release build at `3c0a7eb`, `episode_check_bench` #1601): 12.16 s scan, 10.3 MB `VmHWM` growth and 4 µs cancel latency; window cancel under 1 s in `tools/alpha/k1.py` (#1319) and `menus.rs` unit tests `toolbar_has_episode_check` and `effects_menu_has_episode_check` (#1569). Item issue #1049 is still open and can close.
- **L0 Speech-to-text library, model and licence.** The choice is written in docs/v1/architecture.md, section "Transcription (L0, 2026-11-03)": candle runner, Whisper base multilingual. The weights' licence is in THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt, written by tools/notices.py (tools/test_notices.py). Done check: v1.md has the L0 row (my ruling of 2026-11-12: "Yes, spec 75 items"). Section L's intro: a pure-Rust MIT or Apache runner; the weights' licence checked and written into the notices file before the model ships. Recorded: docs/v1/architecture.md (Transcription section) and THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt ("Speech-to-text model weights"). Item issue #1056 is closed.

**Remains:**

#### K4: Suggested fix per finding, with Before/After (partial)

- **On main:** K4.1 (#1242): a LoudnessDrift finding's value keeps its sign, positive when louder than the file and negative when quieter (crates/core/src/check/drift.rs, the `Finding::value` doc in check/mod.rs; test in crates/core/tests/episode_check.rs). check::fix::suggest in crates/core/src/check/fix.rs maps a finding to a fix (K4.3), but nothing applies it yet, and Clipping and True peak still get none. check::list::after_fix drops a fixed row and shifts the later ones (K4.7). My answer for Clipping and True peak is Q10 in docs/v1/questions-and-answers.md (K4.2): DeClipper, and the Dynamics limiter with lookahead. The tools exist: effects amplify, normalize, high_pass, dc_offset in crates/core. Plosive findings now grow across adjacent low-dominant unclipped frames (`GROWTH_SHARE`, `GROWTH_DOMINANCE` in `crates/core/src/check/plosive.rs`, K4.4a #1602) so a high-pass fix covers the whole burst and a rescan reports none. Merged 2026-11-12: K4.1 (#1242); K4.3 (#1275); K4.7 (#1282); K4.2 (#1292). Merged 2026-11-20: K4.4a (#1602).
- **Done check:** Applying a fix is one History step; Undo restores the samples bit-identical; Before/After switches with no gap, by ear.
- **Recorded so far:** `crates/core/tests/episode_check.rs` plosive burst-growth tests (K4.4a, #1602). Fix application and Before/After are not recorded yet.
- **Item issue:** #1054
- **Start in:** K4.4 (#1585, moved from `medium` to `hard` after the pre-K4.4a run exposed the plosive burst tail) is ready now that K4.4a (#1602) and K3.5 (#1621) are merged. apply goes beside suggest in crates/core/src/check/fix.rs: Operation::Effect through effects::find and effects::apply (crates/core/src/effects.rs), Operation::Delete through doc::edit::delete. fix.rs's ChannelMask is a plain u64, while those two take the ChannelMask of crates/core/src/doc/edit.rs, so apply converts. The test file crates/core/tests/episode_check_fix.rs is new. K4.5 calls noise::capture and noise::set_current (crates/core/src/noise.rs) before the noise_reduction effect. K4.6 follows Q10: diagnostics::declip::declip (crates/core/src/diagnostics/declip.rs) and the dynamics effect (crates/core/src/effects/dynamics.rs) replace the Kind::Clipping | Kind::TruePeak arm of suggest, and every_named_kind_has_one_fix changes with them. K4.8 to K4.10 need the findings panel (K3.5).

#### L1: Transcribe the open file, saved with it (partial)

- **On main:** the model side of L1 (#1218): `Transcript` and `Word` with JSON read and write (crates/core/src/transcript.rs); the sidecar `sidecar_path`, `save_beside`, `load_beside`; DocState carrying the transcript and `History::push_transcript` (crates/core/src/doc.rs, doc/history.rs); `transcript::mel::windows` and `log_mel` (transcript/mel.rs); the `SpeechEngine` trait, a `FakeEngine` and the runner `transcribe` (transcript/run.rs). Tests: crates/core/tests/transcript.rs, transcript_run.rs, transcript_state.rs. The window side of a fake run (L1.11): action `transcript.run` ("Transcribe"; test transcript_run_is_in_the_table in crates/core/src/actions.rs), the worker `TranscriptRun` in apps/wavcraft/src/transcript_run.rs (WAVCRAFT_FAKE_SPEECH=1 picks the fake engine; tests a_run_with_fake_engine_ends_with_transcript and a_cancelled_run_returns_none), progress and Cancel in the status bar, the History step "Transcribe", control command `TRANSCRIPT run|cancel|state`, and tools/alpha/l1.py, which has not been run yet. The model catalogue and downloader are in `crates/core/src/transcript/models.rs`: `WHISPER_BASE` pinned to revision `e37978b` with SHA-256 `verify` (`sha2`, L1.7 #1557) and streaming HTTP `download` behind `wavcraft-core`'s `whisper` feature (`ureq` =2.12.1 + `CDLA-Permissive-2.0` allowed in `notices.py`, L1.8 #1625). The pure cross-attention DTW word-alignment functions (`median_filter`, `dtw`, `token_starts`, `words`) adopted from FaderFrame `a5bb740` (MIT) are in `crates/core/src/transcript/align.rs` (L1.10a, #1628). Open and Save / Save As in `apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs` load and write `<file>.transcript.json` beside the audio without overwriting the main sidecar on Save Selection As (`apps/wavcraft/src/l1_12_sidecar_tests.rs`, L1.12 #1618). Missing: forwarding the `whisper` feature in `apps/wavcraft/Cargo.toml` and CI (L1.18), pause-aligned overlap windows and silence gating (L1.20), the candle Whisper runner and attention hookup (L1.9, L1.10), preserving a background run across edits and dropping weights after inference (L1.19), the release-build run of `tools/alpha/l1.py` (L1.11 Left), the Edit menu entry and download confirmation dialog (L1.13), and `--measure` plus the real-episode runs (L1.14–L1.17). Merged 2026-11-12: L1.1–L1.6 (#1218); L1.11 code (#1325). Merged 2026-11-17/22: L1.7 (#1557), L1.12 (#1618), L1.8 (#1625), L1.10a (#1628).
- **Done check:** 1-hour episode transcribes in under 15 minutes on the 64 GB Linux tablet and fits the 8 GB Mac's budget (I7), both measured. English accuracy good enough to edit from, by eye on a real episode.
- **Recorded so far:** `crates/core/src/transcript/models.rs` (3 unit tests, L1.7 #1557), `crates/core/tests/transcript_models.rs` (7 tests under `--features whisper`, L1.8 #1625), `crates/core/src/transcript/align.rs` (5 unit tests, L1.10a #1628), and `apps/wavcraft/src/l1_12_sidecar_tests.rs` (7 tests, L1.12 #1618). The `tools/alpha/l1.py` release-build run and the real Whisper measurements are not recorded yet.
- **Item issue:** #1057
- **Start in:** L1.11's release-build run of `tools/alpha/l1.py`, L1.18 (`apps/wavcraft` `whisper` feature forwarding + CI check), L1.19 (edit-resilient `TranscriptRun` + `catch_unwind` + weight drop) and L1.20 (pause-aligned overlap windows + silence skip) have no open blockers and can be taken now. L1.9 is on the critical path to 1.0 (blocking L1.10, L1.13–L1.17, L2.8–L2.9 and L5.7) and still awaits the shared Whisper crate split out of FilmCraft's speech crate.

### Alongside: the MP3 encoder

The `craft-mp3` crate (its own repo) is a dependency of wavcraft-core: `v1.1.0` (gapless), pinned by rev to the commit that tag names (`456f049`), as the SoundCraft crates are pinned. What is left is to wire it in.

**Done when** a 1-hour episode exports to MP3 and passes A3's Done check.

**Remains:**

#### A3: Save As MP3 with the craft-mp3 encoder (partial)

- **On main:** wavcraft-core depends on craft-mp3 v1.1.0, pinned in the root Cargo.toml by rev 456f049, and THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt lists it (A3.1). #1326 moved the pin from v1.0.0 to v1.1.0, as I chose on 2026-11-12; its proof records cargo tree printing craft-mp3 v1.1.0 and tools/notices.py --check passing. crates/core/src/save.rs uses it: save_mp3(doc, target, options, progress) streams 64 K-frame blocks into craft-mp3's Encoder (write_mp3_stream) through the atomic temporary file and writes the Info tag over the start, and save() with Mp3 uses Mp3Options::default() (A3.4, #1375). The encoded stream is held in memory until the tag is written (about 86 MB per hour at 192 kbps), never the source audio. crates/core/src/mp3_options.rs has Mp3Options, MP3_BITRATES, MP3_DEFAULT_BITRATE (192) and mp3_check (A3.2). SaveFormat::Mp3 in crates/core/src/save.rs parses 'mp3', is named MP3 and accepts every sample type (A3.3). A save is refused with SaveError::Unsupported, before any file is made, for a rate other than 32000, 44100 or 48000 Hz and for a channel count mp3_check refuses. With options.mono a stereo document is mixed down to one channel, (L+R)/2, block by block inside the stream_samples loop of save_mp3, so the source audio is never held whole; a mono document with mono set is encoded as it is (A3.5, #1464). The window's save_with calls save_mp3 for a .mp3 path (A3.6, #1503) with the new app state mp3_bitrate (192) and mp3_mono; the control line SAVE <path> bitrate=<kbps> channels=1|2 sets both, sticky like quality=, and the refusal text for an unknown extension lists .mp3. SaveArgs in crates/automation/src/lib.rs gained bitrate: Option<u32> (one of MP3_BITRATES) and channels: Option<u16> (1 or 2), read by take_bitrate and take_channels; parse_save_args skips both tokens so they never land in the path, and SaveArgs::line writes them back. Headless::save writes .mp3 through save_mp3 with them too: mp3_bitrate (192) and mp3_mono are private fields set by SAVE bitrate= and channels=1|2 and kept for later saves, as the window keeps them, and a bit rate that is not in MP3_BITRATES answers ERR naming 'bitrate=' with no file made (A3.11, #1551). The MCP save tool has no bitrate or channels argument, so through MCP a .mp3 is saved with what the last SAVE line set (192 kbps stereo by default). Both `v1::save_formats_in` (Save As file-dialog filters) and `export_window` (Save As / Export window) in `apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs` offer `SaveFormat::Mp3` with the 14 bitrates (192 preselected), a Mono checkbox (disabled for mono files), disabled sample-type and dither controls, and CBR size estimates driven by `mp3_bitrate` and `mp3_mono` (`SAVEOPTS dialog mp3` and `SAVEOPTS estimate mp3`, A3.7 #1558; its debug-build look by eye is still owed). Known limits (tracked by A3.13): non-MP3 sample rates such as 88.2 or 96 kHz fail `mp3_check` unless converted first, and `save_mp3` holds the encoded MP3 byte stream in RAM until the Info frame is patched (~432 MB on a 3-hour 320 kbps export). The brief is `docs/v1/tasks/mp3-encoder-brief.md`. Tests: crates/core/tests/mp3_options.rs the_v1_choices, crates/core/tests/save_mp3.rs, crates/core/tests/987_save_format_parse.rs, apps/wavcraft/src/a3_mp3_tests.rs and tools/abx/abx.test.js. Merged 2026-11-12: A3.1 (#1321, #1326), A3.2 (#1327). Merged 2026-11-13: A3.3 (#1331). Merged 2026-11-16: A3.11 (#1551), Headless's MP3 save (above); A3.4 (#1375), the writer; A3.5 (#1464), the mono mixdown (above); A3.10 (#1385), the size estimate: estimate_size answers the constant-bit-rate size for Mp3 through the new estimate_mp3_size(doc, Mp3Options) in save.rs (frames times bit rate times 125 divided by the rate, at 192 kbps, not exact, with the refusals of mp3_check), and the Save As size line says "constant bit rate" for MP3 (format_size_label in app.rs); A3.6 (#1503), the window's save path (above); A3.12 (#1412), the ABX page tools/abx/index.html (two file pickers, Play A, B and X, Stop, 16 trials, the "n of 16" score and a download of abx-log.csv) over the pure functions in tools/abx/abx.js (verdict, makeTrials, scoreOf, logCsv). The page has not been run in a browser yet.
- **Done check:** A 1-hour episode exports, plays in Apple Podcasts' validator and in a browser, and one more listener and I can't tell it from the WAV at 128 kbps mono speech by ear.
- **Recorded so far:** The Done check is not recorded. A3.4 (#1375) has crates/core/tests/save_mp3.rs mp3_is_a_save_format, a_stereo_tone_round_trips_at_192 (10 s of a 1 kHz stereo tone at 44.1 kHz and 192 kbps: the file is 238000 to 243000 bytes, the Info tag counts 441000 to 444500 samples, it reopens stereo at 44100 Hz with 441000 to 444500 frames and its middle-second RMS is -9.03 dBFS within 0.5 dB), a_failed_mp3_save_leaves_the_target_whole, a_96k_file_is_refused and mono_option_on_stereo_is_refused_until_a35; mp3_save_is_refused_as_unsupported_until_its_writer_lands is gone. A3.5 (#1464) replaced mono_option_on_stereo_is_refused_until_a35 with crates/core/tests/save_mp3.rs mono_from_stereo_is_the_average (left 1 kHz at -6 dBFS, right silent, 5 s at 48 kHz, 128 kbps mono: the file is 78400 to 81600 bytes, reopens with 1 channel at 48000 Hz, and its middle-second RMS is -15.05 dBFS within 0.5 dB) and mono_on_a_mono_document_is_unchanged (a 1-channel document with mono set reopens with 1 channel and a middle-second RMS of -9.03 dBFS within 0.5 dB). ### Stage 6: Design pass (the last stage before 1.0)

When every stage above is done, the Project Manager runs the design skill on the running build, screen by screen, against the one design system all my apps share. Each difference that matters (layout, spacing, type, colour, components, states) is filed as an issue labelled `design-pass` and `kind:ui`, linked to its mockup under `docs/design/<date>/`. M3's named sizes and spacing must be in first, so the pass changes values in one place.

**Added 2026-11-16 at my request: audit every panel and menu and clean up the interface, for beauty and for function.** The pass covers every surface a user can reach, not only the main window: each panel, each menu and submenu, each dialog, the toolbar and tools, the editor, both faces, and the empty, busy, error and disabled states. Each surface is judged on two axes:

- **Beauty:** alignment and spacing on M3's named metrics, type sizes and weights, colour and contrast against the shared design system (light and dark), icon and glyph consistency, visual hierarchy (what the eye lands on first), and density at 100 % and 200 % scale.
- **Function:** Audition's names for menus, panels and commands (AGENTS.md rule 7), every control reachable by keyboard and shown with its shortcut, sensible defaults, clear disabled and busy states, error text that says what to do, no dead or duplicate entries, and nothing that needs hunting for (Stage 4's Done when).

Like the rest of Stage 6, these steps wait for every stage above (R04): S6.1 is blocked by Stages 1 to 5b, so the surfaces it captures are in their 1.0 form (Stage 4b's K1.7 changes the menus and toolbar, and M1 to M4 add the Episode Map and the faces), and the audits come after it, so the clean-up is not redone. Each finding becomes its own `design-pass` issue (one change, one check), filed from the audit report rather than listed here.

**Done when** every panel, menu, dialog, face and state is audited for beauty and function, every `design-pass` issue is closed or moved after 1.0, and I sign off on S6.9. Then [PIPELINE.md](PIPELINE.md) moves to Prototype / Beta, and I call 1.0.

## Horizon features: bringing WavCraft to life (proposed, owner ruling Q13)

Added 2026-11-26: six standout capabilities built directly out of WavCraft's existing seams — the copy-on-write block `Store`, `DocState`, `Episode Map`, `Stft`, `Transcript`, `Episode Check`, `craft-mp3` and `wavcraft mcp`. Each turns WavCraft from a fast clone of a waveform editor plus bolted-on panels into a unified **sound-and-words instrument** where audio blocks, spectral energy, spoken words and loudness proofs know about each other natively.

Per [SPEC.md](SPEC.md) ("A changed Must or Won't sets the approval back to 'not yet'"), `P1` to `P6` are **proposed** here and excluded from the 1.0 step totals above until I rule on Q13 (pull selected items into 1.0, or schedule them as the 1.1 wave right after 1.0).

### P1: Block-Anchored Words (drift-proof, copy-pasteable transcript)

Every other text-based audio editor stores a transcript as flat timestamps that rot as soon as audio is pasted, rearranged or edited while transcription is still running. Because WavCraft's audio lives in immutable content-addressed blocks (`BlockRef` in `crates/core/src/doc.rs`), each `Word` can carry an optional `Anchor { block_id, block_offset }` alongside its document frame span:

- **Copy and Paste carry their words:** copying 30 seconds of an intro or pull-quote to the start of an episode (or `Copy to New` / `Paste to New`) carries its exact words and timings automatically with zero re-transcription.
- **Zero cumulative drift:** after 500 filler cuts and crossfades, word times resolve directly from the live `Channel` block table.
- **Edit freely while transcribing:** transcription hashes/anchors against immutable source blocks, so the user can trim dead air and intro music immediately while Whisper finishes the rest of the hour in the background.

| Step | What | Verify | Size | Kind | By | Blocked by | Issue |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
